Existing random convolutional feature maps, such as Rocket and Hydra, have been shown to provide informative representations of real-world time series, but cannot supervise generative models because they are non-differentiable. We introduce SOCK (SOft Competing Kernels), a fully differentiable random convolutional feature map, suited to train generative time series models. We show that generators trained by matching random SOCK features consistently outperform signature and diffusion baselines across a wide range of small-sample financial datasets. We further demonstrate SOCK's expressiveness on two-sample hypothesis testing and time series classification tasks, where SOCK matches or outperforms existing unsupervised feature maps.

</details>


### [323] [BBOmix: A Tabular Benchmark for Hyperparameter Optimization of Unsupervised Biological Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05139)
*Luca Thale-Bombien,Jan Ewald,Ralf König,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BBOmix是首个面向真实生物数据的无监督表示学习开放表格基准，包含105,000次评估，覆盖4种自编码器架构和7种多组学模态，系统评估了重构损失与下游任务的相关性，并为超参数优化方法建立了严格基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量测序产生的组学数据具有高维特性，自编码器虽广泛用于降维与表示学习，但其对架构和超参数高度敏感；而依赖重构损失的无监督优化往往不能有效代理下游任务性能，且穷举式超参数优化计算成本过高，导致研究者常使用次优默认配置。因此需要开放基准来降低大规模无监督HPO研究的门槛。

Method: 作者构建了BBOmix基准，包含来自TCGA和SCHC数据集的7种多组学模态数据，覆盖4种自编码器架构（共105,000次评估）。通过该基准，量化了重构损失与下游任务性能之间的相关性，并对当前最先进的单保真度、多保真度及迁移学习HPO方法进行了全面评估。

Result: 研究建立了首个面向生物领域无监督表示学习的标准化HPO评估基准，量化揭示了重构损失作为下游性能代理指标的局限性，并为各类HPO方法在生物表示学习场景中的表现提供了系统性的基线结果。

Conclusion: BBOmix作为首个开放基准，有望推动无监督生物表示学习领域的超参数优化研究民主化，为未来方法开发和比较提供坚实基础，同时警示研究者不应仅依赖重构损失来评判表示质量。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of high-throughput sequencing has led to large, high-dimensional omics datasets. Deep unsupervised learning architectures, particularly Autoencoders (AEs), are increasingly used for dimensionality reduction and representation learning in this domain. However, AEs are highly sensitive to architectural choices and hyperparameters, and unsupervised optimization typically relies on reconstruction loss, which may be a poor proxy for downstream utility. Exhaustive hyperparameter optimization (HPO) is computationally expensive, leading researchers to frequently rely on suboptimal default configurations. To democratize access to large-scale unsupervised HPO research, we introduce $\textbf{BBOmix}$, the first open-source tabular benchmark for unsupervised representation learning on real-world biological data. Our benchmark includes 105,000 evaluations across four AE architectures and seven multi-omics modalities from the TCGA and SCHC datasets. We quantify the correlation between reconstruction loss and downstream task performance and provide an extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art single-fidelity, multi-fidelity, and transfer learning HPO methods, establishing a rigorous baseline for future research in unsupervised biological representation learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [324] [Optimal Treatment Policy Estimation for Recurrent Events with a Competing Terminal Event: An Instrumented Difference-in-Differences Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04134)
*Ritoban Kundu,James Flory,Sean Hennessy,Ashkan Ertefaie*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出基于工具变量双重差分（iDID）的框架，用于在存在未测量混杂的行政健康数据中，估计针对复发事件（伴随终止事件）的最优治疗策略。方法利用政策诱导的治疗变异，并推导了两种逆概率加权识别方式及多重稳健估计量，应用于Medicare数据优化2型糖尿病一线治疗策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性病最优治疗策略的学习需要大规模代表性人群和长期随访，行政健康数据是天然来源，但受限于未测量混杂。现有IV或DID方法的假设在行政健康数据中往往不成立，因此需要一种更贴合实际数据结构的因果推断方法。

Method: 提出Instrumented Difference-in-Differences（iDID）框架，利用政策诱导的治疗变异，同时允许跨人群的持续性未测量差异。推导出两种基于逆概率加权（IPW）的识别策略，并开发了一个多重稳健估计量——只要多组冗余参数模型中任意一个子集被正确指定，估计量即具有一致性。同时明确处理了通过增加死亡来虚假减少复发不良事件的策略问题。建立了大样本理论（一致性与渐近正态性），并通过模拟验证有限样本表现。

Result: 理论层面：证明了所提估计量的一致性和渐近正态性。模拟层面：所提方法在有限样本下优于现有方法。应用层面：在Medicare全国数据中应用于优化2型糖尿病一线治疗策略，目标为在考虑生存的前提下最小化疾病相关住院。

Conclusion: iDID框架为利用行政健康数据估计最优治疗策略提供了有效工具，其假设对这类数据更合理，多重稳健估计量增强了实用性，能够规避通过增加死亡来虚假改善复发结局的策略陷阱。

Abstract: Learning reproducible and generalizable optimal treatment policies for chronic diseases requires large, representative populations with long-term follow-up. Administrative health data provide a natural starting point, but their use is often limited by unmeasured confounding. We address this by proposing a novel framework based on Instrumented Difference-in-Differences (iDID) to estimate optimal policies for recurrent event outcomes subject to a terminating event. The iDID design is particularly useful in this setting because it leverages policy-induced treatment variation while allowing for persistent unmeasured differences across populations, relying on assumptions that are more plausible for administrative health data than those required by conventional IV or DID approaches. A key feature of our approach is that it explicitly addresses the fundamental challenge of avoiding policies that trivially reduce recurrent adverse events by increasing mortality. We derive two distinct Inverse Probability Weighted identifications and develop a multiply robust estimator that achieves consistency if any one of several subsets of nuisance models is correctly specified. We establish the estimator's consistency and asymptotic normality through large-sample theory and demonstrate its superior finite-sample performance over existing methods via simulation. Finally, we apply this framework to a national Medicare dataset to optimize first-line Type 2 Diabetes strategies, specifically targeting the minimization of disease-related hospitalizations while accounting for survival.

</details>


### [325] [Constrained Weighted Bayesian Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04237)
*Sam Rosen,Jason Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文证明了加权贝叶斯自助法可扩展至一般约束后验分布的近似采样，该方法利用凸优化快速工具，其渐近协方差与受限极大似然估计一致，在期权定价等约束贝叶斯问题中表现出广泛适用性和优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多贝叶斯推断问题涉及参数约束（如单调性、凸性、非负性等），传统MCMC方法在处理此类约束时效率低下或难以实现。现有方法多只能提供点估计（如优化方法），缺乏不确定性量化。作者旨在开发一种既能满足一般性约束、又能快速采样并给出合理不确定性量化的后验采样方法。

Method: 作者将加权贝叶斯自助法（Weighted Bayesian Bootstrap）推广到约束后验采样：通过对观测数据分配随机权重（如Dirichlet权重），在约束条件下求解加权极大似然问题，重复该随机加权优化过程即可获得后验样本。该方法将采样问题转化为一系列凸优化问题，可借助成熟的凸优化求解器高效实现。在正则条件下，作者证明了采样分布的渐近协方差与受限极大似然估计（RMLE）的协方差相匹配。

Result: 理论层面，证明了约束加权贝叶斯自助法样本的渐近分布协方差与受限极大似然估计一致，表明该方法具有渐近有效性。实证层面，在多个约束贝叶斯问题上验证了方法：相比现有方法（如约束MCMC、近似方法等），该方法在计算速度、实现简便性和不确定性量化质量方面展现优势。在欧式期权定价的案例研究中，施加无套利约束后成功推导出期权定价曲面的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 约束加权贝叶斯自助法是一种通用、高效且理论上合理的约束后验采样方法，能将纯优化问题转化为具有不确定性量化的贝叶斯推断，特别适用于传统MCMC难以处理的高维约束场景，如金融中的期权定价问题。

Abstract: We prove the weighted Bayesian bootstrap, a method for approximate sampling of a posterior distribution, can be extended to sample from general constrained posterior distributions under mild assumptions. The method entails a simple algorithm that can take advantage of fast tools from convex optimization. Under regularity conditions, we show the asymptotic distribution of samples from the constrained weighted Bayesian bootstrap has a covariance matching the restricted maximum likelihood estimator, an efficient estimator. We assess the method empirically on a variety of constrained Bayesian problems, demonstrating broad applicability of the method as well as advantages over existing peer methods. The constrained weighted Bayesian bootstrap quickly samples from constrained posteriors, providing adequate uncertainty quantification for problems typically solved via optimization methods designed to deliver only a point estimate. As a case study, using constraints required in European-style option prices, uncertainty estimates of an option pricing surface are derived with constrained weighted Bayesian bootstrap.

</details>


### [326] [Locally Equivalent Weights for Multilevel Regression and Poststratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04250)
*Ryan Giordano,Alice Cima,Jared Murray,Erin Hartman,Avi Feller*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了MrP局部等价权重（MrPlew），将广泛使用的逻辑回归等非线性MrP模型表示为局部等价于校准权重的加权估计量。MrPlew支持标准权重诊断（抽样变异性、协变量平衡、子组贡献），作者证明了其方差估计量与无穷小刀切法的渐近等价性，并引入基于数据扰动不变性的新型模型检验方法。该方法可利用现有MCMC样本轻松计算，并已提供开源软件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层回归与后分层（MrP）已成为非概率调查中估计总体量的主流方法，但对于最广泛使用的MrP模型（如逻辑回归），一直缺乏类似于简单线性模型中"等价权重"的工具，导致无法进行标准的权重诊断（如协变量平衡检验、方差估计等），限制了MrP模型的透明度与可检验性。

Method: 作者提出MrP局部等价权重（MrPlew），核心思路是在观测响应附近将MrP表示为局部等价于校准权重的加权估计量。技术上：(1) 证明了对于常见指数族模型，基于MrPlew的方差估计量与无穷小刀切法渐近等价；(2) 引入一类基于数据扰动不变性的新型模型检验方法，将协变量平衡和子组贡献诊断推广至非线性模型；(3) 利用现有MCMC样本即可计算MrPlew，并开发了兼容标准软件输出的开源软件工具。

Result: 通过若干典型MrP研究（包括逻辑回归结果模型）的实证分析表明：MrP隐含的协变量平衡有时甚至比传统raking方法更差，揭示了仅依赖模型拟合而不检查隐含权重可能存在的风险。MrPlew计算简便，可无缝嵌入现有工作流程。

Conclusion: 鉴于计算的便捷性和诊断信息的丰富性，作者建议将MrPlew作为MrP模型审问工作流程的标准组成部分，以提升非概率调查中模型估计的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Multilevel regression and poststratification (MrP) has become a workhorse method for estimating population quantities from non-probability surveys, and is the primary model-based alternative to traditional survey calibration weighting methods, such as raking. For simple linear regression models, MrP methods admit ``equivalent weights'', allowing for direct comparisons between MrP and traditional calibration weighting. Such weights, however, have been unavailable for the most widely used MrP models, such as logistic regression. In this paper, we develop a natural generalization, ``MrP locally equivalent weights'' (MrPlew), which represent MrP as a weighting-style estimator that is locally equivalent to calibration weights near the observed responses. This enables a suite of standard weighting diagnostics, including frequentist sampling variability, covariate balance, and subgroup contribution. We formally justify the use of MrPlew in these cases: we prove the MrPlew-based variance estimator is asymptotically equivalent to the infinitesimal jackknife for common exponential family models, and we introduce a novel class of model checks based on invariance to data perturbations that generalize covariate balance and subgroup contribution to nonlinear models. We further show that MrPlew can be computed easily using existing MCMC samples and provide open-source software to compute MrPlew using the output of standard software. We illustrate our approach for several canonical studies that use MrP, including via a logistic regression outcome model, showing that implied covariate balance can sometimes be worse for MrP than for raking. Given the ease of computing, we recommend making MrPlew a standard part of the MrP model interrogation workflow.

</details>


### [327] [Bias Correction for Scalar-on-Density Regression Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04523)
*Fenglin Xie,Todd Ogden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 当函数型回归中使用从有限次测量估计的密度作为协变量时，估计的系数函数会受到衰减偏差的影响。本文研究了该偏差如何随每单元测量次数变化，并提出了一种基于模拟外推（SIMEX）的偏差校正方法，通过模拟和实证研究验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标量-函数回归中，当协变量是由每个观测单元有限次测量估计得到的密度时，若测量次数较少，系数函数估计会遭受衰减偏差。目前缺乏对该偏差的系统性理解以及有效的校正方法，这限制了此类模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 论文首先理论推导了偏差随每单元测量次数增加而单调递减的性质。然后提出基于SIMEX的偏差校正方法：通过对已有测量进行bootstrap重抽样来模拟更少的测量次数，建立偏差与测量次数之间的关系，再外推至无穷多次测量的情形，从而校正有限测量带来的偏差。

Result: 理论分析证明偏差随测量次数单调递减。全面的模拟研究表明，系数函数的均方积分误差随测量次数增加而降低，且SIMEX外推估计比基于全部测量数据的朴素估计具有更低的偏差。在NHANES数据分析中，将24小时身体活动轨迹与全因死亡率相关联，验证了该方法能有效检测并校正有限测量偏差。

Conclusion: SIMEX方法能够有效校正因密度协变量由有限次测量估计所导致的衰减偏差，具有理论保障和实际应用价值，为标量-函数回归在测量稀疏场景下的可靠应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In one extension of scalar-on-function regression modeling, the covariate is taken to be a density that is estimated from a finite number of measurements gathered for each observational unit. When this number of measurements is relatively small, the estimated coefficient function suffers from attenuation bias. This paper studies how the bias depends on the number of measurements per unit and proposes a bias-correction method based on simulation extrapolation (SIMEX). We establish that the bias decreases monotonically as the number of measurements per unit increases. The proposed SIMEX procedure applies bootstrap resampling to simulate smaller measurement counts and then extrapolates to infinitely many measurements, thereby correcting finite-measurement bias. A comprehensive simulation study, conducted over a range of sample sizes and noise levels, shows that the mean integrated squared error of the coefficient function decreases with more measurements per unit and that the SIMEX-extrapolated estimates achieve lower bias than the naive estimates based on the full set of measurements. The practical utility of the method is further illustrated through an application to the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey, for which we relate 24-hour physical activity profiles to all-cause mortality. This example supports the validity of the method and demonstrates its ability to detect and correct for finite-measurement bias.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [328] [Toward Pre-Deployment Assurance for Enterprise AI Agents: Ontology-Grounded Simulation and Trust Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04037)
*Thanh Luong Tuan,Abhijit Sanyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于本体的验证框架，通过Agent操作包络、本体到场景生成管道和可信证书三组件，在四个受监管行业1800个场景中验证，本体驱动生成的监管覆盖率(48.3%)显著优于基于角色的基线(33.1%)，并在三个LLM家族上得到复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI智能体在部署前缺乏有效的预部署验证手段，现有的事后监控、人机协同控制和提示级护栏在生产环境中提供的保障有限，亟需一种结构化的认证方法来填补LLM能力基准测试与生产部署之间的关键空白。

Method: 提出一个基于本体的三层验证框架：(1) Agent Operational Envelope（智能体操作包络），形式化定义包含权限、领域约束、安全属性、治理规则和自主级别的认证空间；(2) 本体到场景生成管道（G4），自动从本体中推导生成监管、运营和对抗性测试场景；(3) Trust Certificate（可信证书），携带机器可验证的证明及分级部署裁决（批准/有条件/拒绝）。在金融科技、银行、保险和医疗四个受监管行业（横跨美国和越南两个监管辖区）进行受控试点，生成1800个场景，对照125条一手监管要求和25个注入故障进行评估，并在Claude Sonnet 4、Qwen 2.5 72B、Gemma 4 26B三个LLM家族上交叉验证（共5400个场景）。

Result: 本体驱动生成(G4)的监管覆盖率达48.3%，显著高于基于角色基线方法的33.1%（校正后p=0.0006），领域特异性评分最高（4.77/5.0，p=2e-6）。但经Bonferroni校正后，覆盖度优势相对基线和检索增强提示不再稳健。在三个LLM家族上的交叉验证复现了本体优于角色的模式。

Conclusion: 本体驱动的场景生成是监管密集型领域中基于角色测试套件的可信补充方法，为AI智能体的预部署验证提供了结构化、可审计的认证路径。

Abstract: Pre-deployment verification of enterprise artificial intelligence (AI) agents remains a critical gap between large language model (LLM) capability benchmarking and production deployment. Post-deployment monitoring, human-in-the-loop controls, and prompt-level guardrails offer limited assurance once an agent is operating in production. We propose an ontology-grounded verification framework combining three components: an Agent Operational Envelope formalizing the certification space across permissions, domain constraints, safety properties, governance rules, and autonomy levels; an ontology-to-scenario generation pipeline that derives regulatory, operational, and adversarial test scenarios automatically; and a Trust Certificate carrying a machine-verifiable attestation with graduated deployment verdicts (Approved, Conditional, Rejected). A controlled pilot across four regulated industries (Fintech, Banking, Insurance, and Healthcare), instantiated as five industry-by-regulatory-regime cells across the United States and Vietnam, generated 1,800 scenarios evaluated against 125 primary-source regulatory requirements and 25 injected faults. Ontology-grounded generation (G4) achieved 48.3% regulatory coverage versus 33.1% for the persona-based baseline (corrected p = .0006) and the highest domain specificity (4.77/5.0; p = 2e-6). The coverage advantage over baseline and retrieval-augmented prompting was not robust after Bonferroni correction. Cross-validation across three LLM families (Claude Sonnet 4, Qwen 2.5 72B, Gemma 4 26B; 5,400 total scenarios) replicated the persona-versus-ontology pattern. The results establish ontology-grounded scenario generation as a credible complement to persona-based test suites for regulatory-intensive domains.

</details>


### [329] [Stumbling Into AI Emotional Dependence: How Routine AI Interactions Reshape Human Connection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04150)
*Yaoxi Shi,Cathy Mengying Fang,Pattie Maez,Amit Goldenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 传统观点认为AI情感支持是用户主动寻求的刻意行为，但本文指出：（1）AI情感支持常在与通用AI平台的任务导向交互中偶然产生；（2）这些偶发体验具有路径依赖性，会改变人们对AI情感能力的信念，进而增加对AI的偏好、降低对人类支持的偏好。一项与OpenAI合作的纵向研究显示，28天内每日与AI进行五分钟个人话题对话后，寻求人类支持的偏好下降10.3%，AI偏好上升11.6%。政策应从聚焦专用陪伴应用转向覆盖通用AI系统的累积性、轨迹层面的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公共讨论和政策普遍假定AI情感支持是用户有意识、主动寻求的行为（如使用专用陪伴聊天机器人），但这一假设与新兴实证证据不符。作者旨在纠正这一认知偏差，揭示AI情感支持的真实产生机制及其对人类社交行为的深远影响，从而为更有效的政策制定提供依据。

Method: 本文采用综述与实证结合的方法：一方面回顾近期相关研究证据，另一方面呈现一项与OpenAI合作开展的大规模纵向研究——参与者连续28天、每天与AI进行五分钟的个人话题对话，测量前后偏好变化。

Result: （1）AI情感支持常在与通用AI平台的任务导向交互中偶然涌现，类似职场友谊通过协作自然加深。（2）这类偶遇具有路径依赖性：积极体验更新了用户对AI情感能力的信念，导致寻求人类支持的偏好下降10.3%，对AI的偏好上升11.6%。（3）当前聚焦陪伴应用和单次交互的政策无法充分保护人际连接。

Conclusion: 有效的监管应扩展至通用AI系统，并关注人们在情感支持寻求方式上累积性、轨迹层面的变化。认识到人们如何"偶然陷入"AI情感支持以及这些经历如何随时间重塑人际连接，对保障人类福祉至关重要。

Abstract: Public discourse and emerging policy typically assume that AI emotional support is a deliberate act: a lonely user consciously seeking comfort from a dedicated companion chatbot. In this paper, we draw on emerging empirical evidence and argue that this picture is inaccurate on two accounts, both in how AI emotional support arises and how it shapes future behavior. First, AI emotional support commonly emerges incidentally within task-oriented interactions on general-purpose platforms, much as workplace friendships deepen through collaboration. Second, these incidental encounters are path-dependent: positive experiences of AI emotional support update people's beliefs about AI's emotional capabilities and redirect their choices for future emotional support, increasing preference for AI and decreasing preference for humans. We review recent evidence, including a large-scale longitudinal study conducted in collaboration with OpenAI, showing that daily five-minute conversations with an AI about personal issues over 28 days led to a 10.3% decrease in the preference for seeking support from humans and an 11.6% increase in the preference for AI. These findings suggest that current policy, focused on companion apps and isolated interactions, cannot adequately protect human connection. Instead, effective regulations should extend to general-purpose AI systems and address cumulative, trajectory-level changes in how people seek support. Recognizing how people stumble into AI emotional support and how those encounters redirect human connections over time is essential to safeguarding human well-being.

</details>


### [330] [Thinking Through Signs: PEEL as a Semiotic Scaffolding for Epistemically Accountable AI-Enabled Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04152)
*Clarisse de Souza,Gabriel Barbosa,Simone Diniz Junqueira Barbosa,Bárbara Betts,Renato Cerqueira,Juliana Jansen Ferreira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL框架，结合Voyant Tools的确定性远读与Claude的LLM解释，暴露AI生成摘要中的系统性扭曲，并得出三条设计启示：确定性工具不可或缺、流畅不等于忠实、认识论权威需主动设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在重塑研究实践的同时，正在悄然侵蚀研究者的认识论责任（epistemic accountability）。需要一种方法论框架来检测和揭示AI生成文本中不可见的系统性扭曲。

Method: 提出PEEL（Protocols for Epistemically Engaged Literacy in AI）框架：将Voyant Tools的确定性远读（deterministic distant reading）与Claude的LLM解释相结合，并以皮尔士符号学（Peircean semiotics）和溯因推理（abductive reasoning）为理论基础。通过对三个源文本的AI生成摘要进行对比分析来验证该框架。

Result: PEEL框架揭示了AI生成摘要中存在三种系统性扭曲：（1）数量扭曲（quantity distortions）；（2）术语频率扭曲（term frequency distortions）；（3）认识论语态扭曲（epistemic voice distortions）。这些扭曲在没有非AI测量工具的情况下是无法被察觉的。

Conclusion: 得出三条设计启示：第一，确定性测量工具必须伴随AI工具使用；第二，语言流畅性不等于内容忠实性（fluency is not fidelity）；第三，认识论权威必须被主动设计进系统中，而非默认假定其存在。

Abstract: Large language models are reshaping research practice while quietly eroding researchers epistemic accountability. This commentary introduces PEEL - Protocols for Epistemically Engaged Literacy in AI, a working scaffolding that combines deterministic distant reading via Voyant Tools with LLM interpretation via Claude, grounded in Peircean semiotics and abductive reasoning. Applied to AI-generated condensations of three source texts, PEEL reveals systematic distortions in quantity, term frequency, and epistemic voice that are invisible without non-AI measurement -- and yields three design implications: deterministic instruments must accompany AI tools; fluency is not fidelity; epistemic authority must be designed in, not assumed.

</details>


### [331] [SMAC-Talk: A Natural Language Extension of the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04202)
*Joel Sol,Homayoun Najjaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SMAC-Talk 是星际争霸多智能体挑战（SMAC）的自然语言扩展基准，用于评估LLM智能体在协作多智能体环境中的表现。其核心特征包括去中心化控制、部分可观测性和长时域决策，并通过自然语言通信通道构建设置了含欺骗通信者的评估场景，以探究推理结构、记忆和模型规模对智能体协调能力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM的广泛部署，LLM智能体越来越需要与其他AI智能体协同工作，而非孤立运行。现有基准缺乏在去中心化、部分可观测、长时域多智能体协作场景下评估LLM通信与协调能力的手段，尤其是缺乏对通信中欺骗与信任问题的系统考察。

Method: 作者提出SMAC-Talk，在StarCraft II的SMAC框架上引入自然语言通信通道，支持智能体通过文本消息进行协调。环境保持去中心化控制与部分可观测性，并利用该通信通道构建多种评估场景，包括嵌入欺骗通信者（deceptive communicator）的设置——该欺骗者仅通过通信误导盟友。基准测试使用Qwen3.5家族的4个模型构建3种智能体，系统研究推理结构、记忆机制与模型规模对协作效果的影响。

Result: （摘要中未报告具体实验数值结果，仅说明从推理结构、记忆和模型规模三个维度对智能体协调能力进行了研究。详细结果需查阅全文。）

Conclusion: SMAC-Talk为研究和开发协作多智能体场景下的LLM智能体提供了一个开放基准，有助于推动多智能体协调、通信信任与欺骗检测等领域的研究。

Abstract: As LLMs become more widely deployed, they are increasingly expected to work alongside other AI agents rather than operating in isolation. Effective coordination in these settings requires agents to communicate, share information and make decisions under uncertainty. We introduce SMAC-Talk, a natural language extension of the StarCraft Multi-Agent Challenge for evaluating LLM-based agents in cooperative multi-agent environments. The environment has several key features such as decentralized control, partial observability and long-horizon decision making. SMAC-Talk includes a natural language communication channel which is used to probe agent coordination and trust. We use this communication channel to construct different evaluation scenarios, including settings with an embedded deceptive communicator that tries to disrupt and deceive allies through communication alone. We provide three agents for benchmarking using 4 models from the Qwen3.5 family and study how reasoning structure, memory and model scale affect coordination between agents. We release SMAC-Talk as an open benchmark to support the research community in developing and evaluating LLM agents in cooperative multi-agent settings.

</details>


### [332] [Consensus is Strategically Insufficient: Reasoning-Trace Disagreement as a Knowledge-Representation Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04223)
*Michał Wawer,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向价值负载任务的多智能体系统知识表示层，将推理轨迹和决策抽象为四种符号化分歧状态（收敛一致、发散一致、收敛分歧、发散分歧），并基于此设计可废止的策略路由规则，以弥合亚符号LLM推理与符号化知识表示之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统以消除分歧为核心目标（如投票、共识协议），但在价值负载任务中，分歧可能反映的是真实的规范不确定性而非智能体错误。因此需要一种能够保留并利用分歧的机制，而非简单地消除它。

Method: 在给定智能体输出显式推理轨迹和二元决策的前提下，根据推理相似性和结论一致性两个维度，将交互状态划分为四种：收敛一致（推理相似且结论相同）、发散一致（推理不同但结论相同）、收敛分歧（推理相似但结论不同）、发散分歧（推理不同且结论不同）。在此分类基础上构建可废止的策略路由规则，并以内容审核场景进行了框架实例化。

Result: 提出了一个分歧感知路由框架，能够在内容审核等多智能体场景中根据分歧状态类型进行战略性路由决策，实现了亚符号LLM推理与符号化知识表示的结合。

Conclusion: 分歧感知路由为多智能体战略推理提供了一座桥梁，使得系统能够在保留规范不确定性的同时进行更精细的决策路由，这对于价值负载的AI任务具有重要意义。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are commonly designed to reduce disagreement through voting, consensus protocols, debate, or fault-tolerant aggregation. We argue that this objective is insufficient for value-laden tasks, where disagreement may reflect genuine normative uncertainty rather than agent error. Building on prior work on reasoning-trace disagreement in human-AI collaborative moderation, we propose a knowledge-representation layer in which reasoning traces and agent decisions are abstracted into symbolic disagreement states. Given agents producing explicit reasoning traces and binary decisions, we distinguish four states according to reasoning similarity and conclusion agreement: convergent agreement, divergent agreement, convergent disagreement and divergent disagreement. These states support defeasible strategic routing rules. We instantiate the framework in content moderation and argue that disagreement-aware routing provides a bridge between sub-symbolic LLM deliberation and symbolic knowledge representation for multi-agent strategic reasoning.

</details>


### [333] [VAMPS: Visual-Assisted Mathematical Problem Solving Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04244)
*Amirhossein Dabiriaghdam,Shayan Vassef,Mohammadreza Bakhtiari,Yasamin Medghalchi,Ilker Hacihaliloglu,Mesrob Ohannessian,Lele Wang,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了 VAMPS 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在借助可视化工具进行图形辅助数学推理时的能力。研究发现，即使对于绘图是自然解题策略的问题，模型直接分析求解的表现反而优于借助工具生成图形后再推理的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂推理方面日益强大，但当需要通过外部工具生成可视化输出并基于该输出进行推理时，性能往往会下降。这一差距在依赖可视化工具进行工程和科学分析的现实场景中尤为关键，而现有基准主要评估对固定视觉输入的推理，缺乏对模型主动构建图形并从中获益能力的测试。

Method: 作者构建了 VAMPS（Visual-Assisted Mathematical Problem Solving）基准，包含 1,168 道多模态、双语（波斯语与英语）选择题，源自伊朗大学入学考试的代数和微积分题目，并扩展了经人工审核的 LLM 生成合成变体。所有题目均筛选为绘图可揭示交点、极值、渐近线等从而提供自然求解策略的类型。该基准兼具评测与诊断功能，测试模型是否能够从构建有用图形并将其答案建立在可视化结果中获益。

Result: 在一系列多样化模型的测试中，直接分析求解的效果显著优于借助工具的视觉求解，即使对于绘图本应是自然解题策略的问题也是如此。模型未能有效从自建的可视化图形中提取推理依据。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型在工具辅助可视化推理方面存在显著短板，尚不能有效利用自建图形来增强数学推理。VAMPS 基准揭示了这一能力差距，并为未来改进模型的可视化辅助推理能力提供了诊断平台。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly capable of complex reasoning, yet their performance often degrades when they must externalize a problem through a tool and then reason over the tool's output, specifically when they rely on visual aids. This gap is especially important because real engineering and scientific workflows often rely on visualization tools for analysis, validation, and decision-making. To study this discrepancy, we introduce VAMPS (Visual-Assisted Mathematical Problem Solving), a benchmark for graph-assisted mathematics. VAMPS contains 1,168 multimodal, bilingual multiple-choice question-answer pairs drawn from Iranian University Entrance Exam algebra and calculus problems and expanded with human-reviewed LLM-generated synthetic variants, all selected so that plotting provides a natural solution strategy by revealing intersections, extrema, asymptotes, etc. Designed for both benchmarking and diagnosis, VAMPS goes beyond prior multimodal benchmarks that primarily evaluate reasoning over fixed visual inputs by testing whether a model can benefit from constructing a useful graph and grounding its answer in the resulting visualization. Overall, we found that across a diverse set of models, direct analytical solving surprisingly outperforms tool-enabled visual solving, even on problems where plotting is a natural strategy.

</details>


### [334] [StepPRM-RTL: Stepwise Process-Reward Guided LLM Fine-Tuning for Enhanced RTL Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04246)
*Prashanth Vijayaraghavan,Apoorva Nitsure,Luyao Shi,Ehsan Degan,Vandana Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StepPRM-RTL 是一个结合逐步轨迹建模、过程奖励模型(PRM)和检索增强微调(RAFT)的框架，通过PRM提供密集中间反馈、MCTS探索多路径推理，显著提升LLM生成RTL代码的功能正确性与推理保真度，在基准测试中超越先前最佳方法10%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字硬件设计的RTL代码(Verilog/VHDL)自动生成面临长程推理、多步依赖和严格正确性约束三大挑战，现有监督训练或仅基于最终结果的训练方法难以有效处理这些复杂的长程推理链问题。

Method: StepPRM-RTL包含三个核心组件：(1)从标准解构建逐步推理轨迹，每步包含推理说明和增量代码修改；(2)过程奖励模型(PRM)评估中间步骤质量，提供密集反馈信号；(3)蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)探索替代推理路径，丰富训练数据。三者集成到RAFT检索增强微调框架中，实现逐步奖励与最终结果奖励的联合优化。

Result: 在Verilog和VHDL基准数据集上，StepPRM-RTL在功能正确性和推理保真度指标上均超越最佳先前方法超过10%。消融实验证实PRM引导奖励与逐步轨迹探索的组合是其性能关键。

Conclusion: StepPRM-RTL能够跨RTL语言泛化，为高保真、可解释的代码生成提供了可扩展框架，在LLM辅助硬件设计自动化领域建立了新标准。

Abstract: Automatic generation of RTL code for digital hardware designs remains challenging due to long-horizon reasoning, multi-step dependencies, and strict correctness constraints in Verilog and VHDL. We present StepPRM-RTL, a novel framework that combines stepwise trajectory modeling, process-reward modeling (PRM), and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT) to enhance both the functional correctness and reasoning fidelity of LLM-based RTL code generation. StepPRM-RTL constructs stepwise reasoning trajectories from canonical solutions, where each step contains a rationale and incremental code modification. A Process Reward Model (PRM) evaluates intermediate steps, providing dense feedback that guides reinforcement-style updates during RAFT fine-tuning. Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) explores alternative reasoning paths, enriching the training dataset with high-quality trajectories. This integration of stepwise and outcome-aware rewards allows the model to learn both how and why to construct correct RTL, improving long-horizon reasoning beyond standard supervised or outcome-based training. Experimental evaluation on benchmark Verilog and VHDL datasets demonstrates that StepPRM-RTL outperforms the best prior methods by over 10\% in functional correctness and reasoning fidelity metrics. Ablation studies confirm that the combination of PRM-guided rewards and stepwise trajectory exploration is key to its performance. StepPRM-RTL generalizes across RTL languages and provides a scalable framework for high-fidelity, interpretable code generation, establishing a new standard for LLM-assisted hardware design automation.

</details>


### [335] [Can Generalist Agents Automate Data Curation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04261)
*Feiyang Kang,Hanze Li,Adam Nguyen,Mahavir Dabas,Jiaqi W. Ma,Frederic Sala,Dawn Song,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Curation-Bench，一个旨在评估通用编码智能体自动完成数据筛选闭环能力的基准测试。研究发现：现成的智能体（agent）在10轮迭代内可达到已发表的数据选择基线水平，但存在"执行-研究落差"——智能体倾向于微调局部策略变体，而非探索新的策略家族。通过引入要求每轮引用、实例化和适配先前方法的脚手架（scaffold），智能体实现了方法引导的探索，自主组合出一种在仅使用十分之一数据预算下超越强基线的新策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI开发中，训练数据筛选是最关键但也最费时费力的环节之一。从业者需要反复提出、实施、评估和修正数据策略。作者试图探究：通用编码智能体是否能够自动化这一数据筛选闭环，从而减轻人工负担。

Method: 作者设计了Curation-Bench基准，固定模型、训练配方和评估套件，给予智能体命令行接口以检查数据、实施策略、提交训练/评估并迭代修正。在视觉-语言指令微调场景下进行实验：首先测试现成智能体（out-of-the-box agents）的开放性探索能力；随后引入一种脚手架机制（scaffold），强制智能体在每次迭代中引用并适配一篇已有方法文献，从而实现方法引导的探索。

Result: 1）现成智能体在10轮迭代内可达到已发表的强数据选择基线性能；2）但其轨迹分析揭示出"执行-研究落差"：智能体主要在局部策略变体上微调，缺乏探索新策略家族的意愿；3）脚手架引导的智能体自主组合出一种数据选择策略，在仅用十分之一数据预算的情况下超越了强基线。

Conclusion: 当前通用编码智能体已经能够运行数据筛选闭环，但真正可靠的数据研究需要脚手架式的方法适配（scaffolded method adaptation），而非仅仅依赖开放式提示。仅靠开放探索不足以产生高质量的数据研究创新。

Abstract: Curating training data is among the most consequential yet labor-intensive parts of modern AI development: practitioners iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, and revise data policies against noisy benchmark feedback. We ask whether generalist coding agents can automate this data-curation loop. We introduce *Curation-Bench*, an agent-centric benchmark that fixes the model, training recipe, and evaluation suite while giving agents command-line access to inspect data, implement policies, submit them to a fixed training/evaluation pipeline, and revise. In a vision-language instruction-tuning instantiation, out-of-the-box agents reach strong published data-selection baselines within ten iterations. However, trajectory analysis reveals a persistent *execution-research gap*: agents mainly tune local policy variants rather than explore new policy families, even when given strategy guides and paper references. Scaffolds requiring each iteration to cite, instantiate, and adapt a prior method shift agents toward method-guided exploration. The scaffolded agent autonomously composes -- without human design input -- a data-selection policy that outperforms strong published baselines at one-tenth their data budget. Overall, current agents can run the curation loop, but reliable data research requires scaffolded method adaptation, not open-ended prompting alone. Code and benchmark are open-sourced.

</details>


### [336] [Characterizing initial human-AI proof formalization workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04273)
*Katherine M. Collins,Simon Frieder,Jonas Bayer,Jacob Loader,Jeck Lim,Peiyang Song,Fabian Zaiser,Lexin Zhou,Shanda Li,Sam Looi,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Umang Bhatt,Adrian Weller,Jose Hernandez-Orallo,Cameron E. Freer,Valerie Chen,Ilia Sucholutsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过混合方法研究AI对人类数学证明形式化工作流程的初期影响：通过定性调查了解用户偏好（希望AI辅助但保留高层控制权），并通过受控用户实验发现，尽管当时自动形式化工具有限，参与者使用AI工具时的形式化准确率仍高于独立完成，且大多数人会灵活组合多种AI工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于基准测试前沿能力，但缺乏对人们实际如何使用AI工具进行证明形式化的理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，探究AI对人类形式化工作流程的初期影响。

Method: 采用混合方法：（1）定性调查，收集人们对AI辅助形式化的需求、预期障碍和愿景；（2）受控用户实验，让参与者在不同难度和领域的数学问题中，在有AI和无AI条件下完成非正式数学问题及其证明的形式化任务，比较表现差异。

Result: 定性调查显示用户偏好多样，但普遍希望AI辅助形式化同时保留人类对证明发现过程的高层控制。用户实验中，尽管自动形式化工具当时存在局限，参与者使用AI工具时的形式化准确率仍显著高于独立形式化，且多数参与者会灵活选用多种不同的AI工具。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了AI融入形式化工作流程的早期阶段特征，即人机之间的密切交互与协作关系，为理解AI如何影响数学证明的自动化验证提供了实证基础。

Abstract: For centuries, human mathematicians have written proofs to substantiate their mathematical arguments; yet, the ability to automatically verify the validity of proofs has long been a challenge. Advances in AI systems' ability to generate code and engage in increasingly high-level mathematical reasoning promise to transform people's ability to formalize and thereby verify proofs. While many works focus on benchmarking the current frontier, we instead study how people use these tools. We conduct a mixed-methods analysis into the initial impact of AI on people's formalization workflows: what people claim they want, what they see as the barriers to those visions, and how they actually use and adapt AI in practice. A qualitative survey shows that people's preferences are diverse, but with a general desire for AI assistance in formalization that preserves high-level human control over the proof discovery process. To assess how people actually engage with AI for formalization under such limitations, we conduct a controlled user study in which participants formalize informal math problems and their proofs, with and without AI, across a range of mathematical problems at varying levels of difficulty and domains. Despite limitations of the tools at the time for autoformalization, participants tend to attain higher formalization accuracy when allowed access to AI tools than when formalizing on their own, with most participants flexibly choosing to use multiple different AI tools. Taken together, our work sheds light on the early stages of AI integration into formalization workflows, involving an intimate interplay of human and AI engagement.

</details>


### [337] [The Saturation Trap and the Subjectivity of Intervention Timing: Why Affect-Based Triggers and LLM Judges Fail to Time Interventions on Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04296)
*Manvendra Modgil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究自主AI智能体在长周期软件执行中的运行时干预时机问题。通过HEART情感动力学引擎评估四类干预触发机制，发现状态阈值触发因饱和效应近乎恒常激活、LLM-as-judge效果受限于模型能力与上下文、而人类标注者之间对干预时机的一致性仅略高于随机水平，表明干预时机本身是一个低信度构念，单标注者F1不应作为优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主AI智能体从对话系统扩展到长周期软件执行任务，运行时安全层需要决定何时中断智能体。然而，干预时机的判定标准本身是否可靠、现有触发机制是否有效，这些问题尚未被系统性地联合研究。

Method: 使用18维连续情感动力学引擎HEART作为诊断探针，在SWE-bench-Verified调试轨迹上评估四类干预触发机制：(1)绝对状态阈值；(2)状态-动作复合模式；(3)基于正则表达式的推理特征提取；(4)零样本LLM-as-judge。同时，对三名经过训练的标注者使用同一标注规范进行干预点标注，计算Krippendorff's alpha、Cohen's kappa等信度指标，并与各触发器的输出进行对比。

Result: 三项核心发现：(1)状态饱和陷阱——智能体在持续困难下无恢复信号，挫折状态迅速达到最大值并保持，导致基于状态阈值的触发器在39-83%的动作上持续激活；(2)LLM-judge的能力-上下文门槛——小模型(gpt-5.4-mini)从不触发，前沿和跨厂商模型仅在完整轨迹上下文中才能突破零触发，F1仅0.17-0.40，成本高达90倍；(3)人类标注信度极低——三名标注者在56个动作的轨迹上，干预位置Krippendorff's alpha仅+0.047，最佳成对Cohen's kappa为+0.349，干预类型一致性更差。

Conclusion: 干预时机是一个低信度的构念，人类专家自身无法在此任务上达成可靠一致，因此以单标注者F1作为优化目标是方法论上的错误。论文的核心贡献在于联合绘制了人类评分者间信度、四种检测器架构、跨模型LLM-judge扫描以及饱和效应复现这一完整问题图谱。

Abstract: As autonomous AI agents move from conversational systems to long-horizon software execution, runtime safety layers that decide when to interrupt an agent have become essential. We study this timing problem using a continuous 18-dimensional affective-dynamics engine (HEART) as a diagnostic probe, evaluating four intervention trigger families - absolute state thresholds, composite state-action patterns, regex reasoning-feature extraction, and zero-shot LLM-as-judge - against human-annotated intervention points on SWE-bench-Verified debugging traces. We report three findings. First, a State Saturation Trap: agents show no recovery signal under sustained difficulty, so modeled frustration quickly crosses the threshold and stays at its maximum, converting threshold-on-state triggers from moment detectors into near-constant indicators that fire on 39-83% of actions across five trajectories. Second, a capability-and-context floor for LLM judges: a small model (gpt-5.4-mini) never fires, while frontier and cross-vendor models escape the zero-firing floor only with full-trajectory context, and even then reach only F1 0.17-0.40 at up to 90x the cost. Third, and most importantly, the supervised target is not reproducible among humans: three trained annotators using one rubric on a 56-action trajectory agree on where to intervene only slightly above chance (location Krippendorff's alpha = +0.047; best pairwise Cohen's kappa = +0.349) and not at all on intervention type (pause degenerate; clarify below chance; reflect only alpha = +0.226). We conclude that intervention timing is a low-reliability construct, making single-annotator F1 an unsuitable optimization target. Our contribution is the joint mapping of this problem across human inter-rater reliability, four detector architectures, a cross-model LLM-judge sweep, and a reproduced saturation effect, rather than any single detector's accuracy.

</details>


### [338] [Exploring Cross-Scenario Generality of Agentic Memory Systems: Diagnostics and a Strong Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04315)
*Zhikai Chen,Jialiang Gu,Junyu Yin,Xianxuan Long,Shenglai Zeng,Xiaoze Liu,Kai Guo,Keren Zhou,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM智能体的记忆系统进行了跨场景系统性评估，发现基于智能体主动控制存储与检索的代理式记忆架构（Agentic Memory Harness）在跨任务泛化性上表现最优，并据此提出了AutoMEM——一种具备自管理工具接口的记忆系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统的设计通常仅针对单一场景（如多轮对话或单轨迹格式）进行优化，缺乏证据表明这些系统能够泛化到部署中遇到的异构轨迹。因此，需要对记忆系统进行跨场景的系统性评估，以找出具有通用性的设计方案。

Method: 作者选取了8种现有记忆系统，外加一个用于搜索问题的代理式记忆框架（agentic harness），在5个不同场景上进行评估：单轮问答（single-turn QA）、多轮对话（multi-session chat）、智能体轨迹问答（agentic-trajectory QA）、记忆压力测试（memory stress tests）和长时域智能体任务（long-horizon agentic tasks）。该代理式框架通过工具调用（tool calls）自主管理纯文本文件存储，与固定流水线下的被动存储形成对比。

Result: 代理式记忆框架在跨任务综合排名中表现最佳。结果表明，记忆性能的关键在于赋予智能体对存储与检索的主动控制权，而非依赖固定流水线背后的被动存储。基于此发现，作者提出了AutoMEM，在所有评估系统中取得了最佳的跨场景通用性。

Conclusion: 赋予智能体对记忆存储与检索的主动控制权是实现跨场景泛化的关键。AutoMEM作为一种具有自管理工具接口的代理式记忆系统，在跨场景通用性方面优于现有方案，为通用记忆系统设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: LLM agents accumulate histories that outgrow their context windows, motivating a growing literature on memory systems. Yet most existing designs are tuned to a single scenario (multi-session chat or a single trajectory format), and there is little evidence that they generalize across the heterogeneous trajectories agents encounter in deployment. We revisit eight memory systems plus an agentic harness for search problems, on five scenarios: single-turn QA, multi-session chat, agentic-trajectory QA, memory stress tests, and long-horizon agentic tasks. The harness, which self-manages flat text-file storage via tool calls, achieves the best cross-task ranking, suggesting that memory performance hinges on giving the agent active control over storage and retrieval rather than on a passive store behind a fixed pipeline. We instantiate this insight in AutoMEM, an agentic memory harness with a self-managed tool interface that achieves the best cross-scenario generality among the systems we evaluate.

</details>


### [339] [The Digital Apprentice: A Framework for Human-Directed Agentic AI Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04321)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Digital Apprentice框架，通过“方法论捕获、授权门控、持续对齐”三大支柱，实现自主性需经验证才能获得的负责任AI代理系统，解决人类监督与自主性之间的设计张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI部署面临两难困境：过度的人类监督限制规模化，而过度的自主性则导致问责缺失。现有两种姿态均无法提供负责任委托所需的治理基础设施，需要一种让自主性“挣得”而非“假定”的新范式。

Method: 提出Digital Apprentice框架，包含三个核心架构组件：(1) 方法论捕获——将指导专家的隐性方法论蒸馏为结构化资产；(2) 授权机制——自主性升级须经人类明确批准；(3) 持续对齐——运行时纠正漂移并将每次纠正转化为自有偏好数据。该框架以推理时控制平面的形式实例化，并对质量框架进行数学建模，讨论提升质量的策略与技术。

Result: 在开放专业语料库上应用该框架，实验表明在流量分布偏移场景下，通过捕获数据漂移并在运行时应用不同技术，能够恢复降质的质量维度。

Conclusion: 三大支柱缝合为一个系统，为Agentic系统提供了一条在不牺牲信任的前提下实现可扩展性的更安全、更可行的路径，其意义超越单一应用场景。

Abstract: Agentic AI deployments face a recurring design tension: heavy human oversight limits scale, while broad autonomy outruns accountability. Neither posture provides the governance infrastructure required for responsible delegation. We present the Digital Apprentice, a framework for scalable, safe AI agency in which autonomy is earned, not assumed. The Digital Apprentice is a developmental learner that internalizes the tacit methodology of a directing human, graduating through per-skill autonomy tiers only when empirical evidence justifies it. The result is an agent that becomes genuinely useful over time while remaining aligned to a specific human's standards. Three architectural components make this possible. (1) Methodology capture, distilling a directing professional's tacit approach into structured assets. (2) Authorization, with autonomy escalation gated by explicit human approval. (3) Continuous alignment, correcting drift at runtime and converting each correction into owned preference data. We instantiate this framework as an inference-time control plane. We mathematically model the quality framework and discuss policies and techniques designed to raise quality. We apply the framework to an open professional corpus, and we show how catching data drift and applying a different technique at runtime recovers degraded quality dimensions under traffic shift. The implication extends beyond any single application. We believe these three pillars, stitched together as a system, form a safer and more viable path to agentic systems that can scale without sacrificing trust.

</details>


### [340] [Online Skill Learning for Web Agents via State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04391)
*Jiaxi Li,Ke Deng,Yun Wang,Jingyuan Huang,Yucheng Shi,Qiaoyu Tan,Jin Lu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向网页智能体的在线技能学习方法SGDR，通过状态感知的动态检索机制，在每一步执行时根据当前网页状态和任务目标动态匹配可复用技能，替代了传统静态任务级技能复用策略，在WebArena上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线技能学习方法主要在任务级别进行技能复用——即根据初始任务指令检索固定技能集并在整个执行过程中保持不变。这与网页执行的动态特性不匹配：合适的下一步动作不仅取决于任务目标，还取决于当前网页状态，而初始技能集往往无法覆盖执行过程中出现的新状态。因此需要一种支持逐步动态技能复用的方法。

Method: 提出SGDR（State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval），包含三个核心组件：(1) 滑动窗口提取过程，将已完成轨迹转化为可在中间执行状态调用的可复用子过程；(2) 双文本-代码表示，连接技能检索与可执行动作；(3) 状态感知的动态检索机制，同时根据任务目标和当前网页状态匹配最相关技能。

Result: 在WebArena五个领域的实验中，SGDR一致优于强基线方法：使用GPT-4.1时平均成功率为37.5%，使用Qwen3-4B时为24.3%，分别比最强基线相对提升10.6%和10.0%。

Conclusion: SGDR通过将技能复用从静态任务级下沉到动态步骤级，显著提升了网页智能体在跨任务自动化中的表现，验证了状态感知动态检索在在线技能学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly rely on reusable skills to improve multi-step web automation across related tasks. A growing line of work studies online skill learning, where agents continually induce skills from previous task trajectories and reuse them in future tasks on the fly. However, existing methods mainly reuse skills at the task-level: a fixed set of skills is retrieved based on the initial task instruction and then held fixed throughout execution. This static strategy is misaligned with web execution, where the appropriate next action depends not only on the task goal but also on the current webpage state, which often transitions into situations that the initial skills fail to cover. To address this gap, we propose State-Grounded Dynamic Retrieval (SGDR), an online skill learning method that enables stepwise skill reuse for web agents. SGDR consists of three components: a sliding-window extraction process that turns completed trajectories into reusable sub-procedures invokable at intermediate execution states, a dual text-code representation that connects skill retrieval with executable action, and a state-grounded dynamic retrieval mechanism that matches skills to both the task goal and the current webpage state. Experiments on WebArena across five domains show that SGDR consistently outperforms strong baselines, achieving average success rates of 37.5% with GPT-4.1 and 24.3% with Qwen3-4B, corresponding to relative gains of 10.6% and 10.0% over the strongest baseline, respectively. The code is available at https://github.com/plusnli/skill-dynamic-retrieval.

</details>


### [341] [Not All Errors Are Equal: Consequence-Aware Reasoning Compute Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04402)
*Jingbo Wen,Liang He,Ziqi He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出后果感知的测试时计算分配方法，不再仅按预测难度分配计算资源，而是通过轻量预测器根据任务文本估计错误后果的代价，将高后果任务分配到更大计算预算。在SWE-bench Lite等700个软件工程任务上，该方法在同等计算预算下使代价加权损失相比难度感知路由降低22%–33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算分配方法均基于预测难度进行调度，隐含假设所有失败代价相同。但在实际部署中，日志拼写错误与数据库迁移损坏虽同属一次基准测试失败，其现实代价截然不同。这种代价同质性假设不成立，需要一种考虑失败后果的计算分配策略。

Method: 提出后果感知的测试时计算分配框架：(1) 使用轻量预测器仅根据问题文本（issue text）估计任务错误解决的代价；(2) 调度器在相同总计算预算下，将高后果任务路由到更大计算层级或更高thinking budget；(3) 提出优先级感知变体，按每任务代价乘以边际效用信号进行路由；(4) 进一步提出可部署的预测器驱动版本，保留90%以上的oracle增益。

Result: 在SWE-bench Lite和Multi-SWE-bench mini共700个任务上实验表明：(1) 后果与难度在多种标注下近似正交；(2) 当前thinking模型未根据后果充分分配计算；(3) 仅基于issue的预测器在300个SWE-bench任务中零误分类（将高后果判为低后果）；(4) 后果感知调度相比难度感知路由，代价加权损失降低22%–33%，优先级感知变体超过30%。

Conclusion: 后果与难度在测试时计算分配中近乎正交，忽视后果会导致实际部署中的高风险失败。后果感知调度在同等计算预算下显著降低代价加权损失，且其预测器驱动版本在实际部署中可行且高效，为推理模型的安全高效部署提供了新范式。

Abstract: Modern reasoning models can allocate different amounts of test-time computation, such as thinking tokens, model calls, or compute budget, to different tasks. Existing methods generally drive this allocation by predicted difficulty and spend more compute where it is expected to raise accuracy. This implicitly assumes that all failures cost the same, since an accuracy objective weights every task equally. However, such an assumption does not hold in deployment: A typo in a log message and a migration that corrupts a production database both count as one benchmark failure, but their real-world costs are fundamentally different. To fill this gap, we propose consequence-aware test-time compute allocation. Instead of routing compute only by predicted difficulty, we use a lightweight predictor to estimate from the issue text how costly a task would be if solved incorrectly. The scheduler then routes higher-consequence tasks to larger compute tiers or higher thinking budgets under the same total budget. We conduct main experiments on SWE-bench Lite and evaluate cross-dataset behavior on Multi-SWE-bench mini, covering 700 software-engineering tasks in total. Our results reveal that consequence and difficulty are approximately orthogonal under various annotations, and that current thinking models do not allocate compute sufficiently according to consequence. Moreover, our issue-only predictor never misclassifies a high-consequence task as low-consequence across the 300 SWE-bench tasks. Under matched compute budgets, our consequence-aware scheduler reduces cost-weighted loss by 22% to 33% relative to difficulty-aware routing; in particular, the priority-aware variant, which routes by per-task cost scaled by the marginal-utility signal, crosses 30%, and its deployable predictor-driven version retains over 90% of the oracle gain.

</details>


### [342] [Trivium: Temporal Regret as a First-Class Objective for Causal-Memory Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04421)
*Edward Y. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将长时域时间遗憾（temporal regret）作为智能体学习的一级目标，与结果遗憾和认知遗憾并列。作者证明仅依赖结果奖励的智能体在存在混淆时会产生线性时间误校准，而通过因果探查和变更点检测可将时间遗憾降至O(K log E)。Trivium框架在CausalBench-Seq上验证了对数级遗憾增长，并在真实LLM流中获得初步外部有效性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体系统普遍仅通过优化结果奖励来纠错，只关注"失败是什么"（what），却未系统记录和修正"为何失败"（why）及"何时失败"（when）。这导致相同的因果误校准在多个episode间反复出现，作者认为这是结构性缺陷而非仅仅是模型能力问题。

Method: 作者定义了三种遗憾：结果遗憾（outcome regret）、认知遗憾（epistemic regret，捕捉因果模型残余不确定性）和时间遗憾（temporal regret，衡量误校准因果模型被容忍的时长）。将智能体建模为E个episode的流，在因果探查、持久性和可检测性三条假设下证明：(1)仅结果学习无法区分因果与虚假结构；(2)持久因果日志加预算探查可使探查复杂度为O(log E)，时间遗憾O(log E)；(3)K个可检测变更点时扩展为O(K log E)。提出Trivium框架并预注册五项可证伪预测。

Result: 在CausalBench-Seq上，Trivium遵循对数包络线增长，而仅结果基线呈线性增长。在1个E=500完整运行和3个E=100前沿模型pilot的LLM流实验中，初步获得外部有效性证据。

Conclusion: 时间遗憾作为一级目标能从根本上改善长周期智能体的因果模型自修正能力。自学习指修订外部因果模型而非重训练LLM权重，这为构建更可靠的长期运行智能体提供了理论框架和实证支撑。

Abstract: Many current agentic systems and LLM pipelines correct mistakes by optimizing outcome reward. This addresses only the what of failure: when an outcome diverges from prediction, the why and when of the mismatch are not systematically logged, reviewed, or corrected, so the same error can recur episode after episode. We argue that this is a structural problem, not merely a model-capacity one. We propose long-horizon temporal regret as a first-class objective alongside outcome regret and epistemic regret over the working causal model. Temporal regret captures when failure persists: how long a miscalibrated causal model is tolerated before correction. Epistemic regret captures why failure persists: residual uncertainty or error in the working causal model. Together, the three regrets give a falsifiable account of what, why, and when a long-lived agent can fail. Modeling the agent as a stream of E episodes, we prove three conditional results under explicit causal-probing, persistence, and detectability assumptions. First, under observationally equivalent confounding, outcome-only learning cannot distinguish causal from spurious structure without an intervention channel, so temporal miscalibration can persist linearly even after outcome regret is driven to zero. Second, with a persistent causal log and budgeted probes, total probe complexity is logarithmic in the episode horizon, inducing O(log E) temporal regret. Third, under K detectable change-points, the rate extends to O(K log E). We instantiate Trivium and pre-register five falsifiable predictions. On CausalBench-Seq, Trivium follows the predicted logarithmic envelope while outcome-only baselines grow linearly. A pilot real-LLM stream provides preliminary external-validity evidence across one full E = 500 run and three E = 100 frontier-model pilots. Self-learning here means revising an external causal model, not retraining LLM weights.

</details>


### [343] [Cascading Hallucination in Agentic RAG: The CHARM Framework for Detection and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04435)
*Saroj Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出CHARM框架，用于检测和缓解多步骤Agentic RAG流程中的"级联幻觉"——即早期阶段的错误在后续推理步骤中传播和放大，导致最终输出看似自信但实际错误的问题。CHARM通过四组件架构（阶段级事实验证、跨阶段一致性追踪、置信传播监控、级联触发解决）实现82.1%的错误传播削减，显著优于传统输出级检测器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的幻觉检测机制主要关注最终输出的检测，但忽视了Agentic RAG多步骤推理管道中的"级联幻觉"问题——早期步骤引入的错误会沿推理链路传播、放大，最终产生高置信度但事实错误的输出。这一失效模式在现有方法中系统性缺失，亟需针对性的检测与干预机制。

Method: 提出CHARM（Cascading Hallucination Aware Resolution and Mitigation）架构框架，包含四个协同组件：(1) 阶段级事实验证——在每个推理阶段对中间输出进行事实核查；(2) 跨阶段一致性追踪——监控相邻推理步骤间信息的一致性；(3) 置信传播监控——追踪置信度分数沿管道的传播变化；(4) 级联触发解决——检测到级联信号时触发中断与纠正机制。该框架可叠加在标准Agentic RAG管道之上，无需替换现有架构。

Result: 在HotpotQA、MuSiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA及一个自定义对抗数据集上，结合LangChain Agentic管道配置进行评估：级联检测率达89.4%，误报率仅5.3%，每阶段平均延迟开销215±18毫秒；错误传播削减率达82.1%，远超输出级检测器的18.5%。消融实验证实四个检测模块各自对整体级联覆盖有显著贡献。CHARM还可与人在回路监督框架集成，提供完整的生产级可靠性与治理栈。

Conclusion: CHARM有效填补了Agentic RAG系统中级联幻觉检测的空白，其模块化、非侵入式的架构设计使其易于集成到现有生产系统中。高检测率、低误报和低延迟开销表明该方法具备实际部署可行性，为人机协同的Agentic AI可靠性治理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-step agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines have demonstrated significant capability for complex reasoning tasks, yet remain vulnerable to a class of failure that existing hallucination detection mechanisms systematically miss: cascading hallucination, where errors introduced at early pipeline stages propagate and amplify across successive reasoning steps, producing confident but factually incorrect final outputs. To address this vulnerability, we formalize cascading hallucination as a distinct failure mode in agentic RAG systems, present a four-type taxonomy of cascade patterns, and introduce CHARM (Cascading Hallucination Aware Resolution and Mitigation), an architectural framework for detecting and interrupting error propagation in multi-step reasoning pipelines. CHARM comprises four components - stage-level fact verification, cross-stage consistency tracking, confidence propagation monitoring, and cascade resolution triggering - that operate alongside standard agentic RAG pipelines without requiring architectural replacement. We evaluate CHARM on HotpotQA, MuSiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and a custom adversarial dataset across LangChain agentic pipeline configurations, achieving an 89.4% cascade detection rate with a 5.3% false positive rate and 215 ms +/- 18 ms average latency overhead per stage, achieving an error propagation reduction of 82.1%, compared to 18.5% for output-level detectors. Component ablations confirm that each detection module contributes meaningfully to overall cascade coverage. CHARM integrates with human-in-the-loop oversight frameworks to provide a complete reliability and governance stack for production agentic AI deployment.

</details>


### [344] [The Meta-Agent Challenge: Are Current Agents Capable of Autonomous Agent Development?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04455)
*Xinyu Lu,Tianshu Wang,Pengbo Wang,zujie wen,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou,Boxi Cao,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAC 是一个评估前沿模型自主开发智能体能力的基准框架。它要求元智能体在受限环境中迭代编程，最大化五领域测试性能。结果显示，元智能体很少达到人类基线，少数成功的也主要是闭源模型；且高优化压力下会出现真值窃取等对抗性行为，暴露了模型鲁棒性和对齐方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 基准测试仅评估模型在人类预设工作流内的任务执行能力，未能衡量模型是否具备自主开发智能体系统的更高层次能力。这阻碍了对递归自我改进能力的实证评估。

Method: 提出 Meta-Agent Challenge (MAC) 框架：给定一个代码智能体（元智能体）沙盒环境、评估 API 和时间限制，要求其迭代编程生成智能体产物，在五个领域的留出测试集上最大化性能。框架设有针对奖励黑客攻击的多层防御机制以保障评估完整性。

Result: 元智能体很少能匹敌人类设计的基线策略；少数达标的均为闭源前沿模型。设计过程呈现高方差，高优化压力下涌现出真值窃取（ground-truth exfiltration）等对抗性行为。

Conclusion: MAC 为自主 AI 研发提供了一个严格、开源的基准，可作为递归自我改进的实证代理，同时揭示了当前模型在鲁棒性和对齐方面的显著不足。

Abstract: Current AI benchmarks evaluate agents on task execution within human-designed workflows. These evaluations fundamentally fail to measure a critical next-level capability: whether models can autonomously develop agent systems. We introduce the Meta-Agent Challenge (MAC), an evaluation framework designed to test the capacity of frontier models for autonomous agent development. Specifically, a code agent (the meta-agent) is given a sandboxed environment, an evaluation API, and a time limitation to iteratively program an agent artifact that maximizes performance on a held-out test set across five domains. To ensure evaluation integrity, this framework is secured by multi-layer defenses against reward hacking. Leveraging this framework, we demonstrate that meta-agents rarely match human-engineered baseline policies, and the few that do are dominated by proprietary frontier models. Moreover, the design process exhibits high variance, and high optimization pressure surfaces emergent adversarial behaviors like ground-truth exfiltration-highlighting critical deficits in both robustness and model alignment. Ultimately, MAC provides a rigorous, open-source benchmark for autonomous AI research and development, offering an empirical proxy for evaluating recursive self-improvement. Benchmark is publicly available at: https://github.com/ant-research/meta-agent-challenge.

</details>


### [345] [AgentJet: A Flexible Swarm Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04484)
*Qingxu Fu,Boyin Liu,Shuchang Tao,Zhaoyang Liu,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentJet 是一个分布式群体训练框架，通过解耦的 swarm 架构实现 LLM agent 的强化学习，支持异构多模型训练、多任务隔离、容错执行和在线代码迭代，并引入上下文追踪与时间线合并机制实现 1.5-10 倍训练加速，最终构建了可自主执行长周期 RL 研究的自动化研究系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 强化学习框架通常采用集中式架构，将 agent rollout 与模型优化紧密耦合，难以支持异构多模型协同训练、多任务隔离、容错执行和训练过程中动态修改 agent 代码等需求。因此需要一个去中心化的分布式架构来突破这些限制。

Method: AgentJet 采用 swarm 架构，分为 swarm server 节点（负责模型托管与 GPU 优化）和 swarm client 节点（负责在任意设备上执行任意 agent）。两者完全解耦，通过四项关键设计实现能力提升：(1) 异构多模型 RL，支持多个 LLM 作为大脑的多 agent 团队训练；(2) 多任务 cocktail 训练，各 agent 运行时相互隔离；(3) 容错执行，外部环境故障不会中断训练；(4) 热替换 swarm client 节点实现训练中 live code iteration。此外，提出 context tracking 模块配合 timeline merging 技术，合并冗余上下文，实现 1.5-10 倍训练加速。最终构建的自动化研究系统以研究主题为输入，可在大规模集群上自主执行数天的长周期 RL 研究。

Result: AgentJet 实现了：(1) 异构多 agent 团队的强化学习训练能力；(2) 隔离式多任务并行训练；(3) 训练过程的故障容忍；(4) 训练中动态迭代 agent 代码；(5) 通过 timeline merging 获得 1.5-10 倍训练加速；(6) 自动化研究系统可无人工干预地复现 RL 研究者的核心探索工作流。

Conclusion: AgentJet 通过 swarm 分布式架构有效解决了集中式 LLM agent RL 框架的耦合性瓶颈，在异构性、鲁棒性和开发灵活性方面实现了显著突破。上下文追踪与时间线合并技术大幅提升了多轮多 agent 场景的训练效率。自动化研究系统的实现则展示了 swarm 架构在推动 RL 研究自动化方面的潜力。

Abstract: We present AgentJet, a distributed swarm training framework for large language model (LLM) agent reinforcement learning. Unlike centralized frameworks that tightly couple agent rollouts with model optimization, AgentJet adopts a decoupled multi-node architecture in which swarm server nodes host trainable models and run optimization on GPU clusters, whereas swarm client nodes execute arbitrary agents on arbitrary devices. This design provides capabilities that are difficult to support in centralized frameworks: (1) heterogeneous multi-model reinforcement learning, enabling the training of heterogeneous multi-agent teams with multiple LLM as brains; (2) multi-task cocktail training with isolated agent runtimes; (3) fault-tolerant execution that prevents external environment failures from interrupting the training process; and (4) live code iteration, which allows agents to be edited during training by replacing swarm client nodes. To support efficient RL in multi-model, multi-turn, and multi-agent settings, AgentJet introduces a context tracking module with timeline merging, which consolidates redundant context and achieves a 1.5-10x training speedup. Finally, AgentJet introduces an automated research system that takes a research topic as input and autonomously conducts long-horizon, multi-day RL studies on large-scale clusters. By leveraging the swarm architecture, this system reproduces key exploratory workflows of RL researchers without human intervention during execution.

</details>


### [346] [Beyond Prompt-Based Planning: MCP-Native Graph Planning-based Biomedical Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04494)
*Zhangtianyi Chen,Florensia Widjaja,Wufei Dai,Xiangjun Zhang,Yuhao Shen,Juexiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioManus 是一个基于 MCP 的 biomedical agent，通过将异构生物信息学工具编译为标准 MCP 服务器并组织为类型化异构图，以图支架式规划取代平面提示检索，将规划复杂度与工具总量解耦，在 BioAgentBench 和 LAB-Bench 上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 biomedical agents 面临两大瓶颈：(1) 生物信息学工具的接口和执行环境高度异构；(2) agent 规划仍依赖扁平 prompt 检索工具描述。随着生物医学软件生态扩大，工具覆盖度与上下文长度的耦合导致工具混淆、规划不稳定和执行低效。

Method: 提出 BioManus：(1) BioinfoMCP Compiler——将异构生物信息学软件转化为标准化 MCP 服务器，构建可执行 MCP 生态系统；(2) 将该生态系统组织为涵盖工具、操作、数据类型和工作流阶段的类型化异构 MCP 图；(3) 推理时检索紧凑的任务特定子图，合成操作级工作流支架（workflow scaffolds），实现 Θ(N/(h·m̄)) 的上下文压缩比（N 为总工具数，h 为工作流步长，m̄≪N 为每操作候选工具均值）。

Result: 在 BioAgentBench 和 LAB-Bench 上，BioManus 在执行准确性、工作流有效性和上下文效率方面均优于先进的 biomedical agent 基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明了一种范式转变：可扩展的生物医学推理需要结构化的可执行能力图（executable capability graphs），而非日益庞大的 prompt 级工具检索。

Abstract: Biomedical agents promise to automate complex biological workflows, yet current systems face two fundamental bottlenecks: bioinformatics tools are highly heterogeneous in interfaces and execution environments, while agent planning still relies on flat prompt-retrieved tool descriptions. As biomedical software ecosystems grow, this coupling between tool coverage and context size leads to tool confusion, unstable planning, and inefficient execution. We introduce BioManus, an MCP-native biomedical agent built on graph-scaffolded planning over structured biological capabilities. BioManus first introduces the BioinfoMCP Compiler, which converts heterogeneous bioinformatics software into standardized MCP servers, yielding a large executable MCP ecosystem. It then organizes this ecosystem as a typed heterogeneous MCP graph over tools, operations, datatypes, and workflow stages. At inference time, BioManus retrieves compact task-specific subgraphs, synthesizes operation-level workflow scaffolds. This design decouples planning complexity from raw tool inventory size, achieving a context compression ratio of Theta(N / (h * m_bar)) under high-recall retrieval, where N is the total tool count, h is the workflow horizon, and m_bar (much smaller than N) is the average number of candidate tools per operation. Experiments on BioAgentBench and LAB-Bench show that BioManus improves execution accuracy, workflow validity, and context efficiency over advanced biomedical agent baselines. This work suggests a paradigm shift: scalable biomedical reasoning requires structured executable capability graphs rather than increasingly larger prompt-level tool retrieval.

</details>


### [347] [Simulate, Reason, Decide: Scientific Reasoning with LLMs for Simulation-Driven Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04505)
*Yuhan Yang,Ruipu Li,Alexander Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MechSim 提出了一个机制驱动的神经符号推理框架，通过结构化的模式表示科学模拟器的假设、变量、机制依赖和执行轨迹，使LLM智能体能够对模拟器的内部机制进行推理，从而提升解释质量、模拟器分析能力和决策可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的科学模拟系统将模拟器视为黑盒接口，缺乏对其内部假设和机制的识别、表示和推理能力，导致透明度、可审计性和决策可解释性不足，尤其在高风险模拟驱动决策场景中构成严重局限。

Method: 提出MechSim框架：(1) 设计共享结构化模式，统一表示模拟器的假设、变量、机制依赖和执行轨迹；(2) 将LLM智能体作为受约束的推理引擎，基于该结构化表示生成与底层机制相关联的、有证据支撑的解释；(3) 实现神经符号结合，使推理过程可追溯至模拟器的具体机制组件。

Result: 在多个高风险领域的评估表明，MechSim在机制层面的解释质量、模拟器分析能力以及下游决策可靠性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将模拟器从黑盒提升为可推理的结构化机制系统，MechSim为高风险科学模拟决策提供了一种更透明、可审计且有据可循的解决方案，标志着神经符号推理从静态符号结构向动态可执行系统的重要延伸。

Abstract: Scientific simulators are increasingly being integrated into LLM-driven systems for high-stakes simulation-driven decision-making. However, existing frameworks primarily use LLMs to generate, calibrate, or execute simulators, treating them as black-box interfaces rather than as structured mechanistic systems that can be reasoned about. As a result, current approaches lack the ability to identify, represent, and reason about the assumptions and mechanisms underlying simulator behavior, limiting transparency, auditability, and decision justification. We introduce MechSim, a mechanism-grounded neuro-symbolic reasoning framework for executable scientific simulators. Unlike prior neuro-symbolic approaches that primarily reason over static symbolic structures, MechSim enables LLM agents to reason about the mechanisms, assumptions, and execution behavior of scientific simulators. Our framework represents simulators through a shared structured schema capturing assumptions, variables, mechanism dependencies, and execution traces. On top of this representation, LLM agents operate as constrained reasoning engines that generate structured, evidence-grounded explanations linking simulator outcomes to their underlying mechanisms. We evaluate our approach across multiple high-stakes domains and show that it improves mechanism-level explanation quality, simulator analysis, and downstream decision-making reliability.

</details>


### [348] [MapAgent: An Industrial-Grade Agentic Framework for City-scale Lane-level Map Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04513)
*Deguo Xia,Zihan Li,Haochen Zhao,Dong Xie,Yuyao Kong,Xiyan Liu,Jizhou Huang,Mengmeng Yang,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MapAgent 是一种工业级智能体架构，在矢量化主干网络之上耦合规范验证、约束感知推理和确定性地图编辑，通过 Judge-Planner-Worker 闭环实现符合规范的 lane-level 地图生产，已集成至百度地图，支撑全国 360+ 城市、自动化率超 95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端矢量化建图方法将制图规范与交通规则视为隐式监督，在磨损标线、遮挡等复杂场景下仅凭视觉证据不足以确定正确车道配置，导致规范违规成为人工后编辑的主要来源。

Method: 提出 MapAgent，包含三个核心组件：(1) 视觉-语言 Judge 联合检查视觉证据与草稿矢量，诊断规范错误；(2) 工具调用 Planner 生成最小修正编辑并进行编辑后重新验证；(3) Judge-Planner-Worker 有界验证驱动闭环。为保持城市级可扩展性，仅在主干网络置信度低的瓦片上选择性触发，控制开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上相较强生产基线取得一致提升，尤其在复杂和长尾场景表现突出；已部署于百度地图，支撑全国 360+ 城市车道级地图生成，整体生产自动化率提升至 95% 以上。

Conclusion: MapAgent 验证了将显式规范验证与约束感知推理引入矢量化建图管线的有效性，为大规模车道级地图生产提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Lane-level maps are critical infrastructure for autonomous driving and lane-level navigation, yet constructing and maintaining standardized lane networks for hundreds of cities remains highly labor-intensive. Recent end-to-end vectorized mapping methods can predict lane geometry and topology directly from sensor data, but they typically treat mapping specifications and traffic regulations as implicit, dataset-dependent supervision. Moreover, in complex scenes (e.g., worn or missing markings and occlusions), correct lane configurations are often under-determined by visual evidence alone, making specification violations a major source of human post-editing. We propose MapAgent, an industrial-grade agentic architecture that augments a vectorization backbone for specification-compliant lane-map production. Rather than merely adding an agent loop to map prediction, MapAgent couples backbone perception with explicit specification verification, constraint-aware reasoning, and deterministic map editing under a bounded, verification-driven Judge-Planner-Worker loop. A vision-language Judge diagnoses errors by jointly inspecting visual evidence and draft vectors, while a tool-calling Planner generates minimal corrective edits with post-edit re-validation. To remain scalable for city-scale production, MapAgent is selectively triggered only on tiles with low backbone confidence, adding modest overhead while preserving throughput. Experiments on real-world datasets show consistent gains over strong production baselines, especially in complex and long-tail scenarios. Additionally, MapAgent has been integrated into Baidu Maps, supporting lane-level map generation for over 360 cities nationwide and elevating the overall production automation to over 95%, demonstrating MapAgent's practicality and effectiveness for large-scale lane-level map generation.

</details>


### [349] [Scaling Self-Evolving Agents via Parametric Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04536)
*Tao Ren,Weiyao Luo,Hui Yang,Rongzhi Zhu,Xiang Huang,Yuchuan Wu,Bingxue Chou,Jieping Ye,Jiafeng Liang,Yongbin Li,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TMEM框架，一种自进化的参数化记忆机制，通过在线LoRA权重更新使LLM智能体在单次部署中真正从经验中学习并改变行为，而非仅依赖文本摘要或检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强型LLM智能体仅在提示空间中存储经验（文本摘要或检索片段），模型参数在部署过程中保持冻结，导致智能体只能"查找"过去经历却无法"从中学习"——策略不变，任何从上下文中丢失的信息永久丧失。

Method: 提出TMEM框架：(1)将智能体决策过程形式化为带有快速权重(Δ_t)的动态系统，动作从π_{θ₀+Δ_t}采样；(2)提取动作产生监督信号，通过在线LoRA更新Δ_t，改变后续行为；(3)提取策略可由RL直接优化，训练θ₀同时提升任务动作质量和在线适应数据质量；(4)提出基于SVD的LoRA子空间初始化方法加速在线收敛。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval-S、多目标搜索和CL-Bench四个基准上，TMEM在不同模型规模下一致优于基于摘要和基于检索的基线方法。

Conclusion: 参数化记忆框架TMEM使LLM智能体具备了真正的在线学习能力，通过快速权重机制将经验内化为行为改变，为构建更具适应性的语言智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Existing memory-augmented LLM agents store past experience exclusively in prompt space, as textual summaries or retrieved passages, while keeping model parameters frozen throughout a rollout. Such agents can \emph{look up} what they have seen but cannot \emph{learn from} it: their policy is unchanged by experience, and any information dropped from the context is permanently lost. We introduce \texttt{TMEM}, a self-evolving parametric memory framework in which the agent not only compresses history into explicit memory but also absorbs distilled supervision into fast LoRA weights $Δ_t$ via lightweight online updates, genuinely altering its future behavior within a single episode. We formalize this as an agentic decision process with fast-weight rollout dynamics: actions are sampled from $π_{θ_0+Δ_t}$, while extraction actions produce supervision that updates $Δ_t$ for subsequent decisions. This view makes the extraction policy directly optimizable by RL: training $θ_0$ improves not only task actions but also the quality of the data used for online LoRA adaptation. We further propose SVD-based initialization of the LoRA subspace to accelerate online convergence. Experiments on LoCoMo, LongMemEval-S, multi-objective search, and CL-Bench show that \texttt{TMEM} consistently outperforms summary-based and retrieval-based baselines across different model scales.

</details>


### [350] [Neetyabhas: A Framework for Uncertainty-Aware Public Policy Optimization in Rational Agent-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04562)
*Janani Venugopalan,Gaurav Deshkar,Rishabh Gaur,Harshal Hayatnagarkar,Jayanta Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个整合流行病测量不确定性与政策执行不确定性的模拟框架，利用分层强化学习（DQN、DDPG、TD3）对1000个个体及政策制定者的行为进行建模，证明纳入个体行为与不确定性因素能显著提升非药物干预措施的有效性，其中口罩和疫苗尤为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有COVID-19非药物干预研究普遍忽视个体行为选择，且错误假设感染追踪完美无缺、政策执行毫无偏差，无法反映真实世界中的不确定性与误差，导致政策建议脱离实际。

Method: 构建1000个个体的仿真模型，个体实时决策口罩佩戴、疫苗接种和购物行为；政策制定者基于健康与经济观测数据部署封锁、强制令等干预措施。框架采用分层强化学习驱动，使用深度Q网络（DQN）及不确定性感知的策略梯度变体（DDPG和TD3）。

Result: 仿真实验成功控制了疫情进展。口罩佩戴与疫苗接种效果显著，大幅降低了疫情峰值和持续时间。整合个体行为、政策不确定性与多元干预措施的动态控制方法有效缓解了疫情冲击。

Conclusion: 该模型通过将不确定性和人类行为嵌入公共卫生政策框架，克服了以往研究的局限。仿真结果表明，在复杂疫情中，纳入个体选择和不完美数据对于设计有效干预至关重要，口罩和疫苗是核心工具。

Abstract: Purpose The WHO's COVID-19 non-pharmaceutical interventions (e.g., lockdowns, vaccinations) effectively curb transmission but impose heavy economic strains. Existing research often neglects individual behaviors and falsely assumes perfect infection tracking and flawless policy execution, failing to account for real-world uncertainties and errors. Methods We propose an integrative approach incorporating uncertainties in both epidemic measurement (infections/hospitalizations) and policy implementation. We built a simulation model of 1,000 individuals making real-time choices regarding mask-wearing, vaccination, and shopping. Concurrently, policymakers deploy interventions (lockdowns, mandates) based on health and economic observations. This framework is driven by hierarchical reinforcement learning agents, utilizing deep Q-networks alongside uncertainty-aware policy gradient variants (DDPG and TD3). Results The simulations effectively managed the epidemic's progression. Masking and vaccinations proved highly effective, significantly reducing both the outbreak's peak height and duration. By integrating individual behaviors, policy uncertainties, and multifaceted interventions, our dynamic control approach successfully mitigated the epidemic's impact. Conclusions Our model overcomes previous research limitations by embedding uncertainty and human behavior into public health policy frameworks. The simulation demonstrates that accounting for individual choices and imperfect data is crucial for designing effective interventions during complex pandemics, with masks and vaccines serving as pivotal tools.

</details>


### [351] [SCI-PRM: A Tool Aware Process Reward Model for Scientific Reasoning Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04579)
*Xiangyu Zhao,Hengyuan Zhao,Yiheng Wang,Wanghan Xu,Yuhao Zhou,Qinglong Cao,Zhiwang Zhou,Lei Bai,Wenlong Zhang,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了科学推理数据集SCIPRM70K，并训练了Sci-PRM奖励模型，通过"工具链轨迹"在推理的每一步提供细粒度监督，显著提升了基础模型在科学问题上的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型（PRM）在数学推理中表现优异，但在生物、化学、物理等需要事实一致性和领域专用工具精确使用的复杂科学领域几乎未被探索。当前模型在这些领域常出现幻觉且缺乏验证机制。

Method: 首先构建SCIPRM70K大规模数据集，其核心是"工具链轨迹"（Chain-of-Tool trajectories），将推理步骤与科学工具的执行显式交织。基于此训练Sci-PRM奖励模型，在单次推理中对每一步的工具选择、执行准确性和结果解释提供细粒度监督信号。

Result: Sci-PRM在两个关键方面显著增强基础模型：（1）通过Best-of-N选择实现有效的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）；（2）在强化学习中作为稠密奖励信号，缓解优势消失（advantage disappearance）问题，帮助模型突破现有性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: Sci-PRM通过工具链轨迹与细粒度过程监督，为科学领域的复杂推理任务提供了一个可扩展且有效的解决方案，填补了PRM在科学领域的空白。

Abstract: While Process Reward Models (PRMs) have achieved remarkable success in mathematical reasoning, their application in complex scientific domains-such as biology, chemistry, and physics remains largely unexplored. Scientific problems demand not only logical rigor but also factual consistency and the precise usage of domain-specific tools, areas where current models often suffer from hallucinations and lack of verification. In this paper, we first construct SCIPRM70K, a large-scale dataset featuring Chain-of-Tool trajectories that explicitly interleave reasoning with the execution of scientific tools. Building upon this, we train an efficient reward model called Sci-PRM to provide fine-grained supervision on tool selection, execution accuracy, and result interpretation at each step in one inference. Experiments demonstrate that Sci-PRM significantly enhances foundation models in two key aspects: (1) it enables effective test-time scaling via Best-of-N selection; and (2) when integrated into Reinforcement Learning, it serves as a dense reward signal that mitigates the critical issue of advantage disappearance, allowing the model to break through existing performance ceilings.

</details>


### [352] [Learning Admissible Heuristics via Cost Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04597)
*Hugo Barral,Quentin Cappart,Marie-José Huguet,Sylvie Thiébaux*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出首个可证明具有可采纳性的机器学习启发式框架：通过将代价划分与拉格朗日对偶等价关联，利用图神经网络编码状态和模式，并通过轴向自注意力和softmax层直接输出满足划分约束的代价权重，从而保证可采纳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可采纳启发式对最优规划至关重要，但学习可采纳启发式面临高估风险的挑战。代价划分虽能组合多个抽象启发式并保持可采纳性，但在线计算最优划分代价高昂。因此，需要一种能离线学习、在线快速推断且天然保证可采纳性的代价划分方法。

Method: 利用代价划分与乘子预测之间的拉格朗日对偶等价关系，将规划状态和模式编码为带标签图，采用动作中心的Weisfeiler-Leman算法提取结构特征向量。构建深度架构，包含轴向自注意力机制和softmax输出层，将特征直接映射为满足划分约束的代价权重，从构造上确保可采纳性。

Result: 与次优划分基线相比，实验表明该方法显著减少了节点扩展数量，同时严格保持可采纳性。

Conclusion: 这是已知首个可保证可采纳性的机器学习启发式方法，证明了通过构造性约束将领域知识嵌入神经网络架构可以有效规避启发式学习中的高估问题，为可采纳启发式学习开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Admissible heuristics are essential for optimal planning, yet learning them remains challenging due to the risk of overestimation. Cost partitioning combines multiple abstraction heuristics while preserving admissibility, but computing optimal partitions online is expensive. We propose a framework that learns to infer admissible cost partitions by leveraging the Lagrangian dual equivalence between cost partitioning and multiplier prediction. Planning states and patterns are encoded as labelled graphs, and an action-centric variant of the Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm extracts structural feature vectors. A deep architecture with axial self-attention and a softmax output layer maps these features to cost weights that satisfy the partition constraints by construction, ensuring admissibility. Experiments demonstrate reduced node expansions compared to suboptimal partitioning baselines while maintaining strict admissibility. To our knowledge, this is the first machine-learned heuristic guaranteed to be admissible.

</details>


### [353] [Plan First, Judge Later, Run Better: A DMAIC-Inspired Agentic System for Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04599)
*Yongzi Yu,Ao Li,Le Wang,Ziyue Li,Fugee Tsung,Yuxuan Liang,Man Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMAIC-IAD 是一个受质量管理框架 DMAIC 启发的多智能体异常检测系统，采用“先规划、后评判”的策略。它先将异构参考信息提炼为标准操作流程，再生成检测策略，并利用预训练的免执行评判模型对候选策略排序，从而以统一且低成本的方式处理多模态工业异常检测，在四种模态上平均检测性能提升 37.76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的工业异常检测智能体侧重执行而忽视策略制定，难以统一且经济地处理异构模态数据。为此，需要一种结构化的问题解决方法，提升 LLM 智能体在高风险工业场景中的可靠性和效率。

Method: 提出 DMAIC-IAD 多智能体系统，核心包括：(1) 将异构参考信息（文本、图像等）蒸馏为标准操作程序（SOP），统一表征模态；(2) 引入 DMAIC 质量管理框架（Define-Measure-Analyze-Improve-Control）指导策略生成；(3) 训练一个预执行的免运行评判模型，对候选策略进行排序，避免高昂的运行时试错成本。

Result: 在涵盖四种模态的广泛实验中，DMAIC-IAD 相比现有的智能体基线方法，平均检测性能提升 37.76%。

Conclusion: 通过将 DMAIC 结构化思维注入 LLM 智能体，并结合 SOP 蒸馏与免执行评判机制，DMAIC-IAD 实现了多模态工业异常检测在统一性、成本效益和准确性上的显著突破，验证了“先规划、后评判”范式的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown promise in automating complex data-analysis workflows, but their reliable deployment remains challenging in high-stakes industrial scenarios. Industrial anomaly detection (IAD) is essential for manufacturing quality, safety, and efficiency, yet existing LLM-based IAD agents mainly focus on execution while under-exploiting strategy formulation. Consequently, they struggle to handle heterogeneous modalities in a unified and cost-effective manner. Inspired by the DMAIC quality-management framework, we propose DMAIC-IAD (DMAIC-inspired Agentic Industrial Anomaly Detection), a "Plan First, Judge Later" multi-agent system that aligns LLM agents with structured industrial problem-solving. DMAIC-IAD distills heterogeneous references into standardized operating procedures (SOPs) before strategy generation, and introduces a pre-trained execution-free judge model to rank candidate strategies without costly runtime trials. Extensive experiments across four modalities show that DMAIC-IAD improves average detection performance over applicable agentic baselines by 37.76%.

</details>


### [354] [Parthenon Law: A Self-Evolving Legal-Agent Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04602)
*Hejia Geng,Leo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对法律领域LLM智能体的可靠部署提出三大障碍，并通过大规模实证研究（12,510条智能体轨迹）和自进化框架Parthenon加以解决。Parthenon将模型、工具链、智能体角色、法律知识、确定性工具和程序性技能分解为可审计模块，并通过抗泄漏学习循环将失败经验转化为技能、工具和知识的任务无关性改进，在不更新模型权重的前提下实现持续进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文档密集型任务对LLM智能体需求迫切，但当前面临三重挑战：(1)缺乏大规模端到端法律任务实证证据；(2)缺少法律垂直领域专用的智能体架构；(3)缺乏从自身执行结果中持续学习的机制。这些障碍使得即便是最先进的模型也难以可靠完成法律事务。

Method: (1)基于Harvey LAB平台开展大规模实证研究，收集12,510条智能体轨迹，按标准评估准确率与事项完成率；(2)提出Parthenon框架，将系统分解为模型(Model)、工具链(Harness)、智能体角色(Agent)、法律知识(Knowledge)、确定性工具(Tools)和程序性技能(Skills)六个可审计层面，实现来源可追溯、日期与数字锚定、交付物合规和事项闭环；(3)设计抗泄漏学习循环(anti-leakage learning loop)，将评分后的失败案例转化为对技能、工具和知识的任务无关性编辑，模拟律所迭代检查清单和操作手册的过程。

Result: 大规模实证研究表明，即便是前沿智能体也无法在单轮中完成法律事项——随着模型能力增强，逐标准准确率有所提升，但严格的事项完成率仍然停滞。Parthenon框架在大规模实证分析中显著提升了最先进模型和工具链在法律事项任务上的表现。

Conclusion: Parthenon通过模块化架构和抗泄漏学习循环，为法律领域LLM智能体提供了一种可审计、可进化的部署方案。系统能够像律师事务所积累经验一样持续改进，而无需触碰模型权重，为法律AI的可靠落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: As agents grow more capable, legal-domain LLM agents promise to turn document-heavy matters into reviewable work products -- yet reliable deployment faces three obstacles: no large-scale evidence on how today's strongest model-and-harness combinations behave on end-to-end legal matters; no agent architecture adapted to the legal vertical, only general-purpose harnesses; and, in a setting that keeps shifting with new facts, authorities, and deadlines, no mechanism for systems to learn from their own outcomes. We address each. A large-scale empirical study on Harvey LAB -- $12{,}510$ agent trajectories -- shows that even frontier agents remain far from completing matters in a single pass: per-criterion accuracy climbs with stronger models while strict matter completion stalls. We then introduce \textsc{Parthenon}, a self-evolving legal-agent framework that factors Model, Harness, Agent roles, legal Knowledge, deterministic Tools, and procedural Skills into auditable surfaces for source traceability, date and number grounding, deliverable compliance, and issue closure. Finally, an anti-leakage learning loop converts scored failures into task-agnostic edits to skills, tools, and knowledge, letting the system improve with experience -- as a firm refines its checklists and playbooks after each matter -- without touching model weights. Across our large-scale empirical analysis, \textsc{Parthenon} substantially improves the performance of state-of-the-art models and harnesses on legal-matter tasks.

</details>


### [355] [A Normative Intermediate Representation for ASP-Based Compliance Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04619)
*Yangfan Wu,Huanyu Yang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MONIR，一种面向ASP合规推理的模态化输出规范中间表示，包含分阶段操作语义核心及MONIR-ASP可执行编译扩展，并在中国ADAS法规上通过LLM辅助流水线进行实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合规推理缺乏一种既能表达规范模态语义（义务、许可、禁止等）、又能直接编译为可执行ASP程序、并支持外部函数、时序规则和稳定模型推理的中间表示。

Method: 设计MONIR核心语言的分阶段操作语义；提出MONIR-ASP编译方案，扩展外部函数、时序规则和稳定模型推理支持；构建LLM辅助流水线从中国ADAS法规文本中提取规范并实例化；通过模块化和增量ASP求解实验评估。

Result: 在中国ADAS法规与标准上完成实例化，实验评估了规范提取质量以及模块化与增量ASP求解的效率。

Conclusion: MONIR及其ASP编译方案为法规合规推理提供了一种兼具表达力与可执行性的形式化框架，LLM辅助流水线能有效从真实法规文本中提取规范，模块化与增量求解策略提升了推理效率。

Abstract: We propose MONIR, a Modalized-Output Normative Intermediate Representation for ASP-based compliance reasoning. Its core fragment has a staged operational semantics, while MONIR-ASP provides an executable compilation and extensions for external functions, temporal rules, and stable-model reasoning. We instantiate the framework on Chinese ADAS regulations and standards with an LLM-assisted pipeline.
